Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on systematics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 973. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 490 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_973(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 973. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^490(S, F) is trivial. QED.
